At the death of this saint, the opinion of all the faithful agreed to elevate him to the patriarchate. He governed his flock in a Christian manner and, in his time, experienced many sorrows. He was elected patriarch in the time of the Emperor Philip (Filibos), who loved the Christians. When Decius (Dakyous) rose up against him, killed him, and sat in his place, he caused great evils to the faithful, and caused many martyrs to perish among the patriarchs, bishops, and all the believers. A crowd fled for fear into the deserts and died there. His guards arrested this father and made many threats against him. Then this infidel perished, and after him reigned Gallus (Gharlalaous). Every day, this emperor directed the persecution against the Christians. After his death, Valerian (Oualaryous) succeeded him, and the persecution reached the Church again. His lieutenants arrested this father, subjected him successively to many punishments, hung him up, and asked him to prostrate himself before the idols. He replied to them: "We prostrate ourselves before the Father and his Son, Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah." The emperor


[p. 227] {#po.16.p227}
استعاد من النفى فقال له بلغنا انك تفرد وتقدس فاجابه نحن ما ندع صلاتنا ليلا ولا نهارا ثم التفت الى الشعب الذى معه وقال لهم امضوا وقدّسوا وان كنت غَائا عنكم بالجيد فانا حاضرا معكم فاغتاظ! الامين واعاد ثفيه فاثار الله على هذا الكافر تاثيرات من البرير فسلم الملك لابنه وكان حكيما حليما فاطلق المعتقلين من المومنين واستعاد من كان فى الذفى فكتب3 للبطريرك ولسائر الاساقفة كتابا بالامان ان يفتحوا ايعهم «لم يمترض لهم احد فعاش هذا الاب بنية حياته فى هدو وسلامة وظبر فى ايام هذا الاب بدع كثيرة في الدين من ذلك ان قوما ظهروا فى اعمال ارايا يروا سو دأيهم ان النفس4 اتموت مع الجد ثم تقوم فى" القيامة معه فجمع عليم مجمع وقطعهم واخرين ايضا” على بداعة اورجانى وسليوس ولما ظهى بولا بطريرك انطاكية السميساصى فكفر بالايمان واجتمع عليه مجمع بانطاكية وام يقدر الاب ان يحض لاجل كبرل فارسل رسالة مملوة من كل
made many threats against him, killed a crowd of people before him, but none of this succeeded in frightening him. Then he exiled him for some time, then allowed him to return from his exile and said to him: "We have learned that you isolate yourself and that you celebrate the offices." The patriarch replied to him: "We will not abandon our prayer either by night or by day," then he turned to the faithful who were with him and said to them: "Go, celebrate the holy sacrifice; if I am absent in body, I am present in spirit." The prince became angry and sent him back into exile. God stirred up against this infidel uprisings of the Barbarians (El-Berber); he handed over the empire to his son. The latter was wise and gentle; he released the imprisoned faithful, brought back those who were in exile, and wrote to the patriarch and all the bishops a letter of safety, to open their churches without anyone opposing it. This saint lived the rest of his existence in tranquility and peace. In his time, many heresies appeared in the religion; thus, one saw in the districts of Arabia (Arabyä) people maintaining, in the error of their mind, that the soul dies with the body and that it rises with it at the resurrection. He gathered a synod against them and excommunicated them. Others also followed the error of Origen (Ouridjanos) and Sabellius (Salyous?). When Paul (Boula) of Samosata (Es-Somaisati), Patriarch of Antioch (Antakyah), appeared, and he was unfaithful to the faith, a council was gathered against him in that city. This father could not


[p. 228] {#po.16.p228}
حكمة ونعمة وين فيها الرأى الصحيح لما بلغ الى! شيخوخة صالحة اتقل الى الاب الرحوم بعد ان اقام على الكرسى الرسولى سبعة عشر سنة صلانه تكون معنا وتحرسنا؟ امين
attend because of his great age; he sent a letter filled with all wisdom and grace, in which he set forth the orthodox opinion. When he reached a respectable old age, he went to the merciful Father after having remained seventeen years on the apostolic seat. May his prayer be with us
اليوم الرابع عشر من برمهات
and protect us! Amen. FOURTEENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 10).
فى5 هذا اليوم استشهد القديس شنودة الببنساوى هذا القديس سعى به الى الامير مكسيموس المتولى من قبل ديقلادبانوسن انه مسيحى فاستحضرا وسأله عن معتقده فاقرك بالمسيح انه7 الح فامر ان يسط على الارض وضرب المطارق التى بيد الجند ففعل به ذلك الى ان تبرًا لحمه فكان دمه يرى جاربا" على الارض ثم جروا برجله9 فارموه0! فى حبس مظلم منتن الرائحة فظهر له ملك الرب مبخايل وقوان!! وصحح جسمه وشفا حراحاته وقال له تقتوؤى ولا تتخفا فلك اكليل ممجّد؟! معد لاحل اتعابك وسوف تال14
On this day died the martyr Saint Sinouti (Chenoudah) of Bahnasah (El-Bahnasaoui). This saint was denounced to the prince Maximus (Maksimous) who governed for Diocletian (Digladyanous). He had him brought and questioned him about his faith. He affirmed that the Messiah was the Truth. The governor ordered him to be stretched on the ground and struck with the clubs that are in the hands of the soldiers. This was done to the point that his flesh was in shreds and one could see his blood flowing on the ground. Then they dragged him by one foot and threw him into a dark and foul prison. The angel of the Lord, Michael (Mikhayil), appeared to him, strengthened him, restored his body safe and sound, healed his wounds, and said to him: "Be strong and do not fear, a glorious crown is reserved for you because of your


[p. 229] {#po.16.p229}
عذابا شديدا! وانا اكون معك كما رسم لى الرب ثم غاب عنه فلما اصبح النبار قال الامير للجند" افتقدواة ذلك الممخالف للملوك ان كان مات فارموا فلما اتوا وجدو واقفا يصلى فاعلموا الامير فاستحضردة وتعب9 من حياته ثم عرانا واي جسمه صحيحا” فت وقال ان هذا سحر قوى ثم امن أن يملق منكس ووقد تحته نار ففعل به ذلك ثم عصر بالبنبازين وضرب ايضا بالسباط ضريا موحعا فلما تعب الامير من عقابه؟ امر. ان يلعب عليه9 بالسيوف وقطع "! فى للكلاب فلم تقريه ولما ان13 كان اللبل اخذوا14 المومنين جسدءة! سرقة ولفوه فى اكفان جدد! وطيبوا باطياب كثيرة”! ثم وضعو فى تابوت ودق؟! صلاته9! تكون معنا امين وفيه ايضا استشهد ل" القديسين اوخانبوس واغاتودرس والبيدبوس هولاء القدسين
sufferings; you will experience a painful punishment, but I will be with you as the Lord has prescribed for me." Then he disappeared. The next day, the prince said to the soldiers: "See if this rebel against the kings is dead, throw him out." When they came, they found him standing, praying. They informed Maximus, who had him brought and was annoyed that he was alive. He had him stripped, saw that his body was intact, and was astonished. "He is a powerful magician," he said. He ordered him to be hung head down and fire to be lit under him. This was done. Then he had him crushed on racks and struck cruelly with lashes. When the prince was tired of tormenting him, he ordered him to be struck with swords and cut into pieces, then he threw him to the dogs, but they did not approach him. At night, the faithful furtively took his body, wrapped it in new shrouds, perfumed it with many aromatics, then placed it in a coffin and buried it. May his prayer be with us! Amen. On this day died the martyrs the saints Eugene (A: Aoukhäanyous,


[p. 230] {#po.16.p230}
كانوا مسيحيين من ابائهم واجدادهم سالكين فى طريق الله ممتأدبين بكل علم الاهى فاختارهم القديس ارموس! بطريرك اورشليم وقدمهم اساقفة على غير كراسى بل ارسايم يكرزون وبشرون3 بالانجيل فشروا فى مدن كثيرة. فمسكيم الكفار فى بعض, المدن فضريوهم4 وهم يجذبونم؟ على الارض الى ان تنبحوا شفاعتهم؟ معنا امين
B: Loudjalyous), Agathodorus (A: Aghatoudoros, B: Aghaboudous), and Elpidius (B: Balibouyous). These saints were Christians from their fathers and ancestors, walking in the way of God, instructed in all divine sciences. Saint Hermus (B: Armous, A: Aryous), Patriarch of Jerusalem (Ourichalim), chose them and consecrated them bishops without sees, but he sent them to preach and announce the Gospel. They announced it in many cities. The infidels seized them in a city, struck them while dragging them on the ground until they died. May their intercession be with us! Amen.
البوم الخامس عشر من برمهات 7فى8 هذا اليوم تنيحت القديسة البارة المجاهدة سارة الراهة9 هذه10 الناسكة كانت من بلاد الصعيد ابنة ابوين عابدين السيد!! المسيح! وكانت لهم ثروةة! جزيلة ولم يكن ليما تنواهاك! فادبا هذ"! القديسة ورياهاً! بكل حكمة”! مسبحية وعلماها الكتابة وكانت مداومة قراة الكتب البيعية8! وخاصة اخبار الرهبان ولاجل مدامبتها فى قراتهم اشتاقت الى السيرة
FIFTEENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 11). On this day died the holy and virtuous athlete Sarah. This nun was from Upper Egypt (Es-Sa'id), daughter of parents pious toward Our Lord the Messiah. They possessed a considerable fortune and had no other children but her. They raised this saint, taught her all Christian wisdom, and taught her writing. She was assiduous in reading ecclesiastical books, and particularly the history of the monks. Following


[p. 231] {#po.16.p231}
الروحانية النسكية فصعدت الى بعض ديارات الرهانات العذارى! الذى قى؟ الصعيد ومكثت فيها سنين تخدم العذارى ثم لبست فيه زى الرهنة فمكثت فيه مجاهدة لشبطان الشهوة ثلثة عش سنة حتى ان الشيطان لما ان غلب منهاة وكل وضجِ من ثاتها ومحتها فى الطبارة قصد4 ان يسقطبا بالكبريا فظبر لبا وهى على سطح قلايتها تصلى وقال لبا ابشرى يا سارة فقد غلبتى الشيطان فاحابته اتنى امرآة ضعيفة ما استطيع؟ ان اغلبه ولا يغلبه وطرده عنى الا7آ المسيح وكانت هذه القديسة تقول للعذارى8 التى معها اقوالا كثيرة" نافعة منها اقالت الى لم اضع رجلى على درحة السلم وارفعها الى الاخرى الا واصوراننى اموت قبل أصعدها!! حتى لا يفرنى! العدو بطول3! الامل وقالت! ايضا جيدا ان يفعل الانسان رحمة ولو انها من اجل_الناس فبمداومتها! سياتى الى رضا الله وقالت اقوالا كثيرة مكتوبة فى اخار المشائخ الرهبان ومكثت هذا القديسة مجاهدة حهادا عظيما وكانت فى قلاية على حافة7!
this continuous reading, she desired the spiritual and ascetic life. She went to a convent of virgins in Upper Egypt and remained there two years serving them. Then she donned the monastic habit and remained there thirteen years struggling against the demon of lust. In the end, Satan (Ech-Chaitan), defeated and struck down by her, crushed by her firmness and her love for purity, resolved to make her succumb through pride. He appeared to her while she was praying on the terrace of her cell and said to her: "Rejoice, Sarah; you have defeated Satan." She replied to him: "I am a weak woman incapable of triumphing over him: he was defeated and driven far from me only by the Messiah." This saint said to the virgins who were with her many salutary words, such as: "I have never set foot on the rung of a ladder and lifted it to the next, without imagining that I would die before having climbed it, so that the enemy might not deceive me with long hopes." She also said excellently: "If a man practices chastity even in the sight of people, by practicing it continuously he will arrive at satisfying God." She said many words that have been written in the accounts of the ancient monks. This saint remained to wage a violent combat; she had, on the edge of the river, a cell where she


[p. 232] {#po.16.p232}
النبر مكثث فيباء ستين. سنة ما ابصرها احدا وما تشرف عليه ولما كبرت هذه القديسة ووصات إلى الثمانين5 سنة انحلت من رباط هذا العالم وذهبت9 الى النعيم الدائم صلاتهاة معنا امين وفيه ايضا تذكار" هلياس الشهيد0! من اهل اهناس صلاته وبركانه تكون معنا امين
remained sixty years without anyone seeing her look above. When she had aged, when she had reached eighty years, she left the monastery of this world and went toward the lasting bliss. May her prayer be with us! Amen. On this day also takes place the commemoration of the martyr Helius of the people of Hnis (Ahnas). May his prayer and his blessings be with us! Amen.
اليوم السادس عشر من برمبات! انى1! هذا اليوم تنيح الاب الطويرك9! القديس انا خاييل بطريرك الاسكندرة هذا الاب كان راهنا بكنيسة القديس ابو مقار وكان عابدا زاهدا فلما تنيح الاب انبا تاودورس ؟! البطريرك مكثت الاباء الاساقفة مجتمعين بمصر اياما كثيرة وهم يتحيروا؟! من"! يصلح لبذه الرتبة الى ان اعلمهم انسان0 قديس بهذا الاب فيه نعمة النبوة فقال اتى !9 كنت بيعة
SIXTEENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 12). On this day died our father, the holy patriarch Khayil, Patriarch of Alexandria (El-Iskandaryah) (743-767). This father was a monk at the church of Saint Macarius (Abou Magar); he was pious, devout. When our father, the patriarch Anba Theodore (Taoudouros) (727-738), died, our fathers the bishops remained long gathered at Misr, not knowing who was suitable for this charge, until a holy man, filled with prophetic grace, made known to them


[p. 233] {#po.16.p233}
القديس ابو مقار اصلّى الى ان حاء صوت من السماء يقول ان خابيل! مستحق لبذي الرّة فشكرودة الاساقفة جميعم وذكروا ان" له فضائل كثيرةٌ واتفقوا عليه واخذوا من متولى مصر" كتاب لبحضروا من الدير ولما ان7 توحهوا الى الجيزة" وجدوا ممائخ الرهبان جاى فى شغل9 فمسكولا وقيدوا واوسمو بطرركا"! فى اليوم السابع عشر من اتوت وكان لاهل" الاسكندرة سنتين ولم تمطن عليهم مطرة الا يسير!! فمطرت فى ذلك اليوم وزانية والثة مطرا غزيرا وجرت فى ايم هذا الاب على المومنين شدائد عليمة؟! وخرجت جماعة من الموبنين هجوا من اللاد المصرية والذى أحصى! ممن كفر بالسيح اريسة وعشرين الف انسا4! وكان البطريرك من ذلك فى5! حزن عظيم! جدا الى ان اهلك الله من كان سب ذلك17 وفى زمان هذا الاب قام على الملكية! بطريرك اسمه قسما وجرت بينه وبين هذا الاب مناظرات فى ذكر الاتحاد بين"! طبيعتى المسيح وكتب الى
this father. "I was praying in the church of Saint Macarius," he said, "when a voice came from heaven, saying: 'Khayil is worthy of this charge.'" All the bishops hastened to thank him, mentioning that there were many virtues in him, agreed upon him, and received from the governor of Misr a letter to have him come from his convent. When they arrived at Giza (El-Djizah), the elderly monks found him going to an occupation. They seized him, bound him, and consecrated him patriarch on the 17th of Tout. For two years, the people of Alexandria had had little rain; but on this day, the next day, and the day after, it rained abundantly. In the time of this father, great persecutions happened to the Christians. A crowd of believers fled from the land of Egypt (El-Misryah), and among those who were unfaithful to the Messiah, twenty-four thousand people were counted. Because of this, the patriarch felt a very great sorrow until God caused the one who was the cause of it to perish. In the time of Khayil, a patriarch by the name of Cosmas (Qosma) arose over the Melkites; there were between him and this father contentions over the mention of the unity between the two natures of the Messiah. Cosmas, the patriarch of the Melkites, wrote to him an


[p. 234] {#po.16.p234}
قسما بطريرك الملكية خطة ان! المسيح بعد الاتحاد طبيعة واحدةة متجسدة كما قال كبراص وكتبت اساقفة خطوطيم بذلك وذكر فى الخط انه لا يجب ان يقال فى السيح من بعد الاتحاد طبيعتين مفترقتين ولا اثنين ولا شخصين فاصطلحا؟ على ذلك واستقرة الامر ان تكون9 كنيسة واحدة ورضى قسما بطريرك الملكية ان يصير اسقفا على مصر واتفق جميعهم على ذلك فافد بينهم رجل ملكى يسمى انسطاطيوس" من" اهل الاسكندرية فانه!! طلب ان يجعل اسقفا فما رضى به فلما لم19 يتفقوا رضى قسطنطين؟! اسقف مص ودخل تحت طاعة انا خاييل واما سيرة هذا الاب فلقى شدائد كثيرة من الملوك4! الظلمة وعوقب عقوبات كثيرةة! بالضرب الكثير والحيسن الطويل والتكبيل9! بالحديد وقدم7! الى ضرب الرقبه عدة مراد والرب ينجيه لرعاية اشعيه ثم اطلق! الى ان9! يمضى الى الصعيد ليتصدق وبجيب له0 فمضى وصنع هناك ايات كثيرة
epistle, according to which, after the union, the Messiah was a single, incarnate nature, as Cyril (Kirlos) said. Bishops wrote mandates on this. It is mentioned in the writing: "It is not fitting to say that after the union, the Messiah is two separate natures, nor two persons." They made peace on this, and the project was established to have only one church. The Melkite patriarch Cosmas agreed to become bishop over Egypt. All agreed on this. But, among them, a Melkite named Anastasius (B: Anastatious, A: Anastatinous), of the people of Alexandria, caused trouble; he wanted to be made bishop. It was not accepted, and, as they did not agree, Constantine (Qostantin) was accepted as bishop of Misr and placed himself under the obedience of Anba Khayil. As for the conduct of this father, he experienced many misfortunes from the tyrannical kings; he underwent many punishments by frequent beatings, long imprisonment, and being put in irons. He was several times on the point of having his head cut off: the Lord saved him to guard his people. Then he was released and went to Upper Egypt (Es-Sa'id) to… He left, performed many miracles there, and brought back to the faith


[p. 235] {#po.16.p235}
ورد كثيرين الى الايمان! ممن خرج من دينه ولما اتصل خر هذا الاب وما جرى عليه لملك الحشة احتمى له وغزا اهل المصر ووصل الى الصعيد وكان معه مأية الف فريس ومأبة الف حمل ونب بلاد كثيرة فلما علم ملك مصر> ان ذلك5 بسبب البطريرك اطلقه وأكرمه وارسل الاب الى ملك الحبشة يبارك عليه وسرفه انه قد تخلص وأمر» ان لا يتعتى؟ الى مكانه بل يمضى؟ الى بلاده فلما قرأ الكتاب ارتحل راجا ولما كمل هذا الاب سعيه وحهاده انتقل الى الرب برحمنا بصلواتهم اجمعين" امين
many of those who had left his religion. When the story of this father and what he had experienced reached the king of Abyssinia (El-Habachah), he became angry and made an expedition against the Egyptians. He arrived in Upper Egypt, having with him one hundred thousand horses and one hundred thousand camels, and pillaged many regions. When the king of Egypt learned that it was because of the patriarch, he released him and honored him. Our father sent to the king of Abyssinia to bless him, inform him that he had been delivered, and prescribe to him not to advance as far as his residence, but to go to his country. When he received the letter, he turned back. When this father had finished his efforts and his struggle, he was transported near the Lord: May He have mercy on us all through his prayers! Amen.
اليوم السابع عشر من برمبات10 اافى19 هذا اليوم تتبح الاب13 البار الصديق العازر حبيبٌ الرب الذى اقامه الرب4! من بعد ان مات بارسة يام هذا البارة! كان من بثى اسرايل وكانت مرا فمريم النى دهنت
SEVENTEENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 13). On this day died our virtuous father, the just Lazarus (El'azar), the friend of the Lord who resurrected him four days after his death. This just man was of the Israelites (Bani Israyil); Martha (Marta) and Mary (Maryam) who anointed the Lord


[p. 236] {#po.16.p236}
السيد بالطيب كانتا! الائنتين اختادة وكانوا جميعهم تلاميذاة لرنا سوع السيح وكان محا فيهم لما فيهم من الفضائلة وكانوا جميعهم لم يتعلقوا بزواج9 فلما حان ان يوفى7 هذا الار الدين المقرر على الطبيعة البشرية وان يشرب الكاس الذى لا بد ان يشريه سا البشر" فاوفاد" فى0! مثل هذا اليوم والرب سبحانه فانه عالم من اول الدهر بان هذا الصديق تنيح فى مثل هذا اليوم وانه سيقيمه وانما تركه يموت ومكث الى ان انت عليه!! ارعة ايام لينتن وقيمه فحينتذ! تعظم!! الابة وعاين ايضا ذاك! فى هذدة! المدة منازل الصالحين والطالحين"! وحينئذآ! اتى الرب له المجد واقامه كما شهد بذلك يوحنا التاولوغس اذ يقول8! ان" الرب دعلا من بعد ارسة ايام فخرج ودلا ورجلا ملفوفين كما تكون الموتى وكان خروجه وهم ملفوفين اية0 مثل اقامته والسبب فى اخراجه وهم ملفوفين وام يامن بحله من داخل !* القى ليتحقق موته ليلا يظن ان ذلك كان حيلة باتفاق
with perfume, were both his sisters. All were disciples of Our Lord Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah who loved them because of the virtues they possessed. None was engaged in the bonds of marriage. When the moment came when this just man had to pay the debt imposed on human nature and drink the cup that all creatures must absolutely drink, the Lord—may He be praised—came to him, for He knew from the beginning of time that this saint would die on such a day and that He would resurrect him. He let him die and remained until four days had passed, that he smelled bad, and that He resurrected him—the miracle would be considerable—and so that, during this time, he saw the dwellings of the good and the wicked. Then the Lord—glory to Him—came and resurrected him as John (Youhanna) the theologian testifies, when he says: "The Lord called him after four days; he came out, his feet and hands wrapped as the dead are." The coming out with hands and feet wrapped is a miracle like his resurrection. The reason for his coming out in this state—now He had not ordered him to untie them inside the tomb—was that his death might be certain, so that one would not believe


[p. 237] {#po.16.p237}
ينما فامري الرب ان يخرج وهو ملفوف على حاله ولباذه عظمت الابة ولو افرضنا ان يقال بعد ذلك انه كان حيًا فكيف كان .يخرج وهو ملفوف البدين والرجلين مغطا للوجه فلرنا2 يسوع المسيح الفادر على كل شى المجد الى الابد امين وفيه4 ايضا تذكار جرجس العابد وبلاسيوسة الشيد ووسف الاسقف بركاتهم الجميع تكون معنا امين
that it was a ruse agreed upon between them. The Lord ordered him to come out thus wrapped so that the miracle would be great. And if one claims to us after that that he was alive, how would he have come out, his feet and hands wrapped and his face veiled? Glory eternally to Our Lord Jesus the Messiah, the Almighty! Amen. On this day also takes place the commemoration of George (Djirdjis) the ascetic, of Blaise (Balasious) the martyr, and of Joseph (Yousof) the bishop: May their blessings all be with us! Amen.
اليوم الثامن عشر من بريبات فى7 هذا اليوم استشهد8 القديس ايسيدراس" رفيق سنا" من الاحناد المجردة مع والى الفرما من اهل دقناس!1 وكان هذا القديس ايسيدراس صديقه يعمل صنعة الصوف وكانا كلاهما يصدقان بما يكسالا فابصرا! فى5! بعض الليالى رونا كان امراة عذرى معها اكاليل تشمهم على رؤسما فلما استيقظاك! علما ببضهما بعض بالمنام ففرحا بذلك فاقناة! وعلما
EIGHTEENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 14). On this day died the martyr Saint Isidore (A: Isidaras, B: Isidarous), companion of Sina, of the soldiers detached with the governor of Pelusium (El-Faramä), of the people of Takinach (Dafnas). This saint worked wool and both gave alms of their earnings. One night, they saw in a dream a virgin holding crowns that she placed on their heads. When they awoke, they made their dream known to each other, they rejoiced, and


[p. 238] {#po.16.p238}
ان الرب قد دعاهما للشبادة! فانوا الى الوالى فحل سنا منطقه؟ وطرحبا قدامه واعثرفا كلاهما بالسسيح فامر باعتقاليما فارسل الرب ملأكه اليهما وعزاهما فارسل الوالى الى سنا الى مدينة الاسكندرية وترك ابسيدراس وبعد هذه رجع والى الاسكندريةة اعاد سناة الى الفرما ففرحا باجتماعهما وق كل منهما ما حجرى عليه فعذهما الوالى عذابا اليما ثم امر ان يحفر حفيرا؟ للقديسن ايسيدراس9! ويرموه فى النار!! فأل الاجناد ان يتصبروا؟! عليه قليل حتى يصلى وصلى! وطاب من المسيح ان يقل نفسة! وتم بجدده ثم تقدم الى الحفرة انار وطرح ذاته فيها فاسام الروح بيد الرب فلم ينال جنده شىء3 من الفساد ولا تغير عن حاله وكانت والدة القديس سنا تبكى عليه لفراقه"! من رفيقه ثم حملوا جسده ووضعوه فى مكان الى ان انقضى الاضطباد وعند ناحة القديس
were convinced that the Lord was calling them to martyrdom. They went to find the governor, Sina undid his belt and threw it before him: both confessed the Messiah. He ordered them to be chained; the Lord sent his angel who consoled them. The governor sent Sina to the city of Alexandria (El-Iskandaryah) and left Isidore. After a certain time, the governor of Alexandria sent Sina back to Pelusium. Both rejoiced at their reunion and told each other what had happened to them. The governor subjected them to painful punishments, then he ordered a pit to be dug for Saint Isidore and for him to be thrown into the fire. He asked the soldiers to wait a little until he had prayed. He prayed and asked the Messiah to receive his soul and to take care of his body; then he advanced toward the flaming pit, threw himself into it, and rendered his soul into the hands of the Lord: his body suffered no damage nor any alteration. The mother of Saint Sina wept over him because of his separation from his friend. They carried away his body and deposited it in a place until the end of the persecution. Upon the death of Saint Isidore, the mother of Saint Sina saw a troop of angels who were carrying


[p. 239] {#po.16.p239}
اسيدراس ! رأت ام القديس سنا جماعة من الملائكة وقد اخذوا نفس القديس أيسيدارس3 وصعدوا با بمجد عليم شفاعته تكون مع بنى المعموديةة امين
the soul of the former and transporting it with great glory. May his intercession be with the Christians! Amen.
اليوم التاسع عش من برمهات
NINETEENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 15).
7فى8 هذا اليوم تنيح القديس المبشر ارسطابولس الرسول هذا القديس كان من جملة السبعين الذين انتخبهم الرب وادسلهم يكرزون9 قبل الام ونال مع التلاميذ ما الود وصحب التلاميذ وخدميم ونادى معهم البشرى المحيية0! ورد كثيرين الى طريق الخلاص ودخل م فى الايمان بالمسيح وعمدهم وخلص نفوسهم افادهم من الوصابا الالبية فاقامته التلاميذ اسقفا على ابرطانيس3! فمضى الها وش فيا وعمدهم ووعظيم! وصنع امامهم ايات كثيرةٌ وناله من اليهود والبونانيين شدائد كثيرةة! وطرد منهم مرارا عديدة؟! ورجم
On this day died the holy bearer of the good news, Aristobulus (Aristaboulos) the apostle. This saint was of the seventy whom the Lord chose and whom He sent to preach before the nations. He received with the disciples what they received, accompanied them, served them, preached with them the life-giving Gospel, brought many back into the way of salvation, brought them into the faith of the Messiah, baptized them, and purified their souls; then he lavished divine recommendations upon them. The disciples consecrated him bishop over Abritanis (B: Abrätäyä). He went into this country, announced the Gospel, baptized the inhabitants, exhorted them, and performed many miracles before them. He experienced many evils from the Jews (El-Yahoud) and the Greeks (El-Younanyin); he was


[p. 240] {#po.16.p240}
بالحجارة ولما أكمل سعيه وتنيح بسلام وقد ذكرا بولس الرسول! فى سالته الى رومية” صلاته تكون معنا امين
chased by them several times and stoned with stones. When he had finished his struggle, he died in peace. The apostle Paul (Boulos) mentioned him in his Epistle to the Romans (Roumyah). May his prayer be with us! Amen.
وفيه ايضا ذكر سبعة من الشهداء وهم الاسكندرون4 والاسكندرون المصرى واغابيوسة من غزة وتيمولاون من البنطس وديوناسيوسن من اطرابلس ورومالوس7 وليسوس" كانا" من قرى مصر هولاء ارتطوا بالمحبة المسيحية واتوا الى والى0! قبسارية فلسطين واعترفوا مامه بالسيع! نلوا أكليل الثهادة فى زمان دبقلاديانون صلواتي3! تكون معنا انين
On this day also takes place the commemoration of seven martyrs. They are Alexander (A: El-Iskandarous, B: Alkandarous), Alexander the Egyptian (El-Misri), Agapius (B: Aghabious, A: Aghabous) of Gaza (Ghazzah), Timolaus (Timoulaous) of Pontus (El-Bontos), Dionysius (Dyounasyous) of Tripoli (Atrabolos), Romulus (Roumalous), and Valesius (A: Balisyous, B: Balyous): these were from the cities of Egypt (Misr). Bound by Christian love, they went to find the governor of Caesarea of Palestine (Qaisaryah Falastin) and confessed the Messiah before him. They received the crown of martyrdom in the time of Diocletian (Digladyanous).
اليوم العشرون! من برمهات
May their prayers be with us! Amen. TWENTIETH OF BARMAHAT (March 16).
تانى6! هذا اليوم "تيح الاب ! اننا خاييل بطريرك الاسكندرية هذا الاب كان ذو خصال
On this day died our father Anba Khayil, Patriarch of Alexandria (El-Iskandaryah)


[p. 241] {#po.16.p241}
جميلة قاله فى بطركيته احزان! عظيمة منها ان اسقف سخا كان جاهلا شريرا؟ محا لمجدةٌ الدنيا وكان بدنواشر4 الذى فى كرسيه بيعة قد جددت تحتاج التكريز فألته اراخنةة اللاد؟ الذين7 اهتموا بالكنيسة ان يسيروا الى الاب البطويرك واساقفة الناحية8 باخذ بركنهم وتكريز بيعتهم ولما ان حاوا بخلاف ما ظن9 الاسقف تركهم فى البيعة وخرج زعم لييتم ليم ولم يتذكر قول الرب ان الذى باج اليه يسير!! فلما1 فات القداس وهو غائب وزاذ فى الغيبة سألت الاساقفة والمشائخ الاب الطريرك ان يقدم القداس ليقروا بعض الكتب الى ان يانى الاسقف ولما لجوا عليه قام بدالة الطريركية وقدم القداس فلما بلغ الاقف ذلك دخل فيه الشبطان! وحاء الى البعة وخطف القريان من على البيكل فكسردة! ورماا وخرج مغضبا فاستدعا الاب قريانا"! اخر وقدس عليه وفى الغد جمع الاساقفة
(881-899). This father was endowed with beautiful qualities, but he experienced great sorrows during his patriarchate. Among others, the bishop of Skhou (Sakha) was ignorant, wicked, loving a base glory. There was at Tanoucher (Danouacher), in his diocese, a church that had been rebuilt and that needed to be consecrated. The magistrates of the country who had charge of the church asked him for permission to go find our father the patriarch and the bishops of the surroundings to receive their blessing and consecrate the church. When they came, contrary to what he thought, he left them in the church and went out, pretending to be busy with them, without remembering the word of the Lord: "Let him who is needed come." As the hour of the mass had passed and as he was still absent, and his absence was prolonged, the bishops and the elders asked our father the patriarch to offer the holy sacrifice to do some readings until the bishop arrived. As they insisted with him, he rose with the insignia of the patriarchate and celebrated the holy sacrifice. When the bishop learned of it, Satan (Ech-Chaitan) entered into him; he came to the church, seized the host on the altar, broke it, threw it, and went out furious. Our father asked for another host and said the mass with it. The next day, he gathered the bishops who were with him, the priests, and the


[p. 242] {#po.16.p242}
الذين! معه والكينة والعلماء وقطع ذلك الاسقف وقدم غيردة فدخل فيه الشيطان فمضى الى متولى مصر ورفع عليه رفاعة وذكن ان عنده مالا كثيرا وان المتولى احضرة وطلب منه مال البيعة واوانها فابى ان يعطيها له وقال له اما جتدى فين يديك وما روحى فيد الخالق فاس بتخشيبه وحيسه فاقام معشقل فوق السنة وهو فى هذ المدة صائم ولا يأكل سوى خزا وباقلا مسلوق وملح وعد ذلك ضمن عليه واخرجه ليزن عشرين الف دنارا
doctors, deposed this bishop, and consecrated another. Satan (Ech-Chaitan) entered into him; he went to find the governor of Egypt (Misr), made reports against the patriarch, and recounted that he possessed great riches. The governor of Egypt had him brought and asked him for the goods and furniture of the church. He refused to give them to him and said to him: "As for my body, it is in your hands; as for my soul, it is in those of the Creator." The governor ordered him to be put on a cross and imprisoned him. He remained chained more than a year; during this time, he fasted and ate only bread, cooked vegetables, and salt. After that, the governor took bail and released him so that he would pay him twenty thousand dinars. He wrote a note to the bail and was able to pay ten thousand.


[p. 243] {#po.16.p243}
وكتب على الضمناء حجة فوزن منها عشرة الف دبنارا واخذ بعضها من الاساقفة ومضها حلا من المومنين ومن رهبان البرية ثم استلف من اهل الاسكندرية واعهم الى ان كمات العشرة الف دنار وكتب خطة لاهل الاسكندرية بالف ديار فى كل سنة واما العشرة الف الاخرى فانه قصد ان يتصدتبها من اهل البلاد يده فمضى الى بلبيس ليبتدى با فاقام فيها يوم فدخل راها مسكينا عليه ثونا عتيقا فاخذ بركة الاب ثم جلس عند الاب بجنب تلميذه وقال له قل للاب لا تضيق صدرك فالى ارعين يوم يتخلص ويأخذ الحجة التى عليه ولا يزن شيئا فاعلم التلميذ الاب فطلب الراهب فما وجده وقبل كمال الارعين .يوم مات الامير مونة سوء وقام ولد بعده فى مكانه فرد الخط على الاب اللطريرك ! وقطعه كما قال الراهب ثم حصل للاب حزنا عظيما على ما جرى منه وعليه ثم تنيح لام بعد
dinars; he extracted a portion of this from the bishops and the other portion he levied upon the faithful and the monks of the desert; then he made the people of Alexandria pay him a quarter of their wealth and wrote them a promissory note for one thousand dinars each year. As for the other ten thousand dinars, he resolved to collect them himself from the people of the country. He went to Belbeis to begin there; he stayed there for one day. A poor monk entered his presence, covered in a worn garment, received his blessing, sat down near the door beside his disciple, and said to him: "Tell the father that his breast should not be oppressed, for within forty days he will be delivered and will take back the writing that binds him, and he will pay nothing." The disciple informed the patriarch, who searched for the monk but did not find him. Before the forty days had expired, the emir died a wretched death; his son succeeded him; he returned the writing to the patriarch and tore it up, just as the monk had said. The father felt great sorrow for what had happened through him and against him. Then he died in peace after having occupied the patriarchate for twenty-nine years. May his prayer be with us! Amen.
ان أكمل فى الطركية سعة وعشرين سنة صلاته معنا امين وفيه" ايضا اقام الرب العازر الصديق من بين الاموات وامن به كثيرا لعظم هذه الاية فله المجد دائما امين
On this day also, God raised Lazarus (El-'Azir) the righteous, and many believed in him because of the greatness of this miracle. Glory to Him eternally! Amen.


[p. 244] {#po.16.p244}
اوفيه ايضا تكريز بيعة القديس انا استقرن بركنه علينا امين اليوم الحادى والعشرون من برمهات
On this day also took place the consecration of the church of Saint Asqaroun. May his blessing be with us! Amen. TWENTY-FIRST OF BARMAHAT (March 17).
فى هذا اليوم حض رنا يسوع المسيبح مع تلاميذه فى بيت عنيا لما كان العازر الذى اقامه من بين الاموات احد المتكيين معه3 وكانت مرا اخته تخدم الجمع الحاشر معه ومريم اخته الاخرى تدهن قدميه بالطيب وتمسحها بشعرهاة فمدحا الب واشار بذكر موته القيب فقال لبا احفظيه ليوم دفنىة وقوله ان المساكين عندكم فى كل حين وانا لست عندكم فى كل حين يرمز بقرب9 صلبه وموته”
On this day, Our Lord Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah was with his disciples in Bethany (Bait 'Anyä), when Lazarus (El-Azir), whom he had raised from the dead, was among those reclining with him. His sister Martha (Martä) was serving the gathering of those present, while his other sister Mary (Maryam) anointed his feet with perfumes and wiped them with her hair. The Lord praised her and alluded to his approaching death by saying: "Keep them for the day of my burial," and also by these words: "There will always be poor among you, but I will not always be among you."
وفيه8 تشاوروال عظماء الكبنة ان يقتلوا العازر لاجل ان كثيرين من عظم الاية0ة
On this day, the chief priests conspired to kill Lazarus (El'-Azir),


[p. 245] {#po.16.p245}
كانوا يومنوا برينا يسوع المسيح! الذى له المجد" والقدرة والمثلمة الى اض الدهر كلبا امين
for, because of the greatness of the miracle, many believed in Our Lord Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah, to whom be glory, power, and greatness
وفيه ايضا ذكر القديسين تاوضوروس وكلياتاوس الشهداء وما شو من الاتعاب على اسم مخلصنا بانواع مختلفة وفى الاخير لوا اكليل الشهداء وورثوا السفارة الابدية فى ملكوت السموات بركاتهم معنا أمين اليوم الثانى والعشرون من برمهات!
until the end of all time! Amen. On this day also takes place the commemoration of the two saints Theodore (Tâoudhourous) and Timothy (Klyatäous), the martyrs, and of what they suffered of torments of every kind for the name of our Savior: in the end, they received the crown of martyrdom and inherited an eternal place in the kingdom of heaven. May their blessings be with us! Amen. TWENTY-SECOND OF BARMAHAT (March 18).
فىية هنا" اليوم تنيح الاب الفديس الروحانى انا كبرلس7 اسقف اورشليم هذا القديسى نشا فى علم البيعة وكان فيه" ماهرا فلما تنيح الاب مكسيموس اسقف اورشليم"! اخيش هذا الاب من بعدد للرتبة!! فرعا شعبه! ابهالًا رعاية واجودها وحرسهم من الذياب المنانية؟! والاريوسية فلما اجتمع المجمع بسرديقى حضر هذا الاب وعاندة!
On this day died the holy spiritual father Anba Cyril (A Kirlos, B Kirlos), bishop of Jerusalem (A Ourichalim, B Yarouchalim); this saint occupied a high rank in the science of the Church and was skilled in it. When our father Maximus (Maksimous), bishop of Jerusalem, died, he was chosen to succeed him in this office. He guarded his flock in the most prudent and noble manner and defended it against the deadly Arian wolves. When the council of Sardica (Sardiqi) met, this father attended it and fought against Arius (Aryous). Acacius (Akäkyous) was deposed from the see of Caesarea (Qaîsaryah);


[p. 246] {#po.16.p246}
اربوس ونفى اكاكيوس من كرسى قبسارية فذهب! اكاكيوس الى قسطنطينوس الملك ابن قسطنطين3 الكبير فشكا له ما جزى عليه من المجمع وبخاصة من هذا القديس كيراص فارسل ونفى كيرلص من اورشليم وجماعة اساقفة من كراسييم4 فجاهد القديس الى طرسوس واجتمع بسلوانس8ة أاستقفها فعاضده" وساعده فلما اجتمع المجمع بسلوكية كان هذا المعظم احد المجتمعين فيه7 ولنوا اكاكيوس فذهب ايضا اكاكيوس الى الملك وعرفه ما جرى عليه واغرى قلبه على الاب كيرلص فارسل ايضا ونفلا ولما مات قسطنطينون وملك ابنه قسطس رد الاب الى كرسيه ورد كل الاساقفة الذين9 نفاهم ابورا فيا فى هدو0! وسلامة بقية ايامه ولما ملك!1 تاوضوسيوس الكبير؟! جمع مجمع! الأية وخسين بمدينة القسطنطينية على مقدونيوسة! حض هذا الاب فيه وقاوم مقدونوس وسلياوس ؟! وابوليناديوس”! ولحنهم واتباعهم واضاف الى الايمانة التى"! وضعها اللاثمئية وثمانية
he left to find the emperor Constantius (A Qostantinous, B Qostantin), son of Constantine (Qostantin) the Great, and complained to him of what he had suffered at the hands of the council, and particularly from Saint Cyril. The emperor sent him into exile from Jerusalem, as well as all the bishops from their sees. The saint went to Tarsus (Tarsous) and joined Silvanus (A Silouânos, B Boulos), bishop of that city, whom he helped and assisted. When the council met at Seleucia (Saloukyah), this venerable saint was among those who were present. They summoned Acacius, who again went to find the emperor, informed him of what had happened, and stirred his heart against our father Cyril. Constantius sent him to be driven from his see; but when he died and was succeeded by his son Constas (read: his nephew Julian), the latter recalled Cyril and all the bishops whom his father (read: his uncle) had exiled to their sees. The patriarch lived in peace and tranquility for the rest of his days. When the great Theodosius reigned, he gathered a council of one hundred and fifty bishops in the city of Constantinople (El-Qostantinyah) against Macedonius (Maqdounyous): this father attended it and fought against Macedonius, Sabellius (Sabalsious), and Apollinaris (B Aboulinäryous, A Abouâtyäryous), cursed their partisans, and inclined toward the


[p. 247] {#po.16.p247}
عشر من عند! نومن3 بالروح القدس الى اخرها وصنف هذا الاب من الروح القدس؟ ميامر عديدة وعظات وصنف+ كتاب يحوى ثمانية عش مقالة وفسر فيه الامانة وهو كتاب نافع جدا مملوا من كل حكمة واقام على الكرسى لئةوثلثين اسنة وتننيح لام صلواتهة معنا امين
faith that the 318 fathers (at Nicaea) had established, according to which we believe in the Holy Spirit. Under his inspiration, the father composed many homilies and sermons; he is the author of a book that contains eighteen catecheses, in which he explained the faith: it is a very useful book, filled with all wisdom. He occupied the see for thirty-three years and died in peace; may his prayer be with us! Amen.
وفيه7 ايضا تذكار نياحة الاب الاسقف المكرم الكامل بكل المعانى صاحب الشبخوخية الحسنة والذكر الجميل ابينا اننا ميخايل اسقف كرسى تقادة الرب يرحمنا بصلاته وطلباته الى الابد امين اليوم الثالث والعشرون من برمهات
On this day also takes place the commemoration of the death of our father, the venerable bishop, accomplished in all mystical sciences, gifted with a beautiful old age and a glorious reputation, our father Anba Michael (Mikhäyil), bishop of Nagadah. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayers and intercessions! Amen. TWENTY-THIRD OF BARMAHAT (March 19).
فى8 هذا اليوم تنيح النبى العظيم دانيال هذا الصديق كان من بيت يوناخير فلما سنا يختنصر اورشليم فى السنة السادسة عشر من مملكته اخذ يوناخير ابن يواقيم ونيه ودائيال
On this day died the great prophet Daniel (Dânyâl). This righteous man was of the family of Younakhir. When Nebuchadnezzar (Bokhtnasr) seized Jerusalem (Ourichalim), in the sixteenth year of his reign, he took Younakhir, son


[p. 248] {#po.16.p248}
ابن عمته! وسبا معهم خلقا عظيماة من بثى اسراييل واخذ كل اموال وجدها فى يت الملك وفى بيت الله كما تنا اشعيا لحزاقياة الملك9 وكان هذا7 النبى صغير السن فاظهر كل سيرة فاضلة كاملة؟ وحات عليه روح الله وتنا فى بابل وفى السنة الرابعة من السبى أصر يختتص رونا صعبة فى منظرها وتاوليا فارحفته جدا ولما اثتبه نسيها فاجمع" كل حكماء بابل فطلب10 منهم!! ما راد وتاويله فلم يعلموا الرونا فصلا عن تفسيرها فرسم بقتليم فلما سمع دائيال السببا لان سيد الشرط اراد قتله وقتل اصحابهة' أمر بتاخير الامير الى .ان ينبى! الملك برونا ثم صلى دائيال هو ورققتهة! الى الله وتضرع"' اليه ان يعرفه تفسير رونا الملك فاوحى الله اليه بها فدخل الى الملك وقص عليه المنام ثم شرحا له7! وين"! فيها انه تنبى عن الملوك الى يملكوا"! بعده وما يحدث بكل واحد منهم
of Jehoiakim (Youâgim), his sons, and Daniel, his aunt's son. He took captive with him a considerable number of people from among the Israelites (Bani Israyil) and seized all the riches that were in the palace and in the temple, just as Isaiah (Ich'ayä) had predicted to King Hezekiah (Hizäqyah). This prophet was young and showed meritorious and accomplished conduct. The spirit of God descended upon him, and he prophesied in Babylon (Bâbil). In the fourth year of the captivity, Nebuchadnezzar had a dream that was terrifying in its appearance and interpretation, which frightened him greatly. When he awoke, he had forgotten it. He gathered all the sages of Babylon and asked them what he had seen and its explanation. As they did not know it, let alone explain it, he condemned them to death. When Daniel was informed of the reason why the chief of police wanted to kill him and his companions, he asked the officer for a respite until he could explain the king's dream. Then he prayed to God, he and his companions, and humbled himself before Him so that He might make known to him the explanation of the prince's dream. The Lord revealed it to him. He entered the king's presence, told him his dream, explained its meaning to him, and demonstrated clearly to him that it concerned the kings who would reign after him and what would happen to each of them.


[p. 249] {#po.16.p249}
شرحا ميخاصا! فاستحسن بخنصر" قول دانيال وسجد له ورسم له بقراين وعطايا وفضله عن كل حكماء بابل ثم رأى الملك2 مناماك اخرة ففسرها" له ايضا وعرفه ان الله7 لاجل تكبرد سيخرجه؟ من بين البشر وسكنه مع الوحوش فى البرارى سبعة اسئين ياكل فيها العشب مثل البهائم ثم يعيده الى ملكه افاتعت_لبونه ثم من بعد موت ينص فر" للطاشار انه ما كتب له الملاك على الحائط عند ما شرب على انية بيت!! الرب فاعلمه؟! ان المكتوب معدود وموزون ومسوط وان تفسيره ان الله قد اعد ملكك3! الى غيرك لانه وزنك ووحدك ناقصا وان ملكك منسطا! الى ملك مادى وماك فارس وتمت نوته واوراه الله فى الرونا الملوك! الاتبين من بعده! الى اخر الزمان والسيح الدجال7! ورأى مجد الله ونظعته وابصر شرف المسيح والاهيته8! وتنا على مجيه وجدد لذلك سنينا فند كملبا كما تنبا وقال ان المسيح ياتى وقتل ولا يكون ليا مخاصا بعد
Nebuchadnezzar received Daniel's word well, prostrated himself before him, assigned him offerings and revenues, and set him over all the sages of Babylon. Then he had another dream that Daniel also explained to him. He made him know that because of his pride, God would cast him out from among human creatures and would make him live for seven years in the desert with wild beasts, eating grass like animals; then that He would restore him to his kingship. The prophecy was fulfilled. After the death of Nebuchadnezzar, he explained to his son Belshazzar (Baltächär) what the angel was writing on the wall while he was drinking from the vessels of the temple. He taught him that what was written was: counted, weighed, dispersed, that is to say: God has transferred your kingdom to another, for He has weighed you and found you wanting: your kingship has been transferred to those of the Medes (Mâdi) and the Persians (Fâris). His prophecy was fulfilled. God made him see in a dream the kings who were to come after him until the end of time, the Messiah and the Antichrist. He saw the glory of God and His power, and contemplated the glory of the Messiah and His divinity. He predicted His coming and determined the years, and upon their fulfillment, he


[p. 250] {#po.16.p250}
وان يت المقدس يخرب بعد قتل المسيح وتبطل القراين والذبايح وقد تم ذلك جميعه وكان بابل صنم وضيفته فى كل يوم اتنى عشر مكيال! دقيق وارعين كيشا وستة اجاجين خمر وكان الملك كورش الفارسى يسجد له كل يوم وكان يظن أن المنم يأكل وشرب ما يوخذ بسبه فنهاد دانيال عن ذلك واعلمه ان الصنم لما يأكل ولما يشرب فغضب الملك واستعلم من كبنة الصنم فقالوا له ان ييل الصنم يأكل ذلك ثم وضعوا الطعام قدام الملك عند المساء وخرجوا فاستحض دانيال رماد ونش فى مكان4 قدام الملك5 ثم اختم؟ الملك المكان بختمه7 فخرج خدام الصنم من سرداب تحت الارض واخذوا ذلك وفى الغعد فتح الملك فما وجد الطعام فصاح عظيما؟ انت بابل9؟ فضحك دائيال واورى الملك اثى مشى الرجال على الرماد فقتل الملك الخدام واكسر"! الصنم وقتل ايضا دائيال الثعيان الذى كانوا اهل بابل يعبدونه ورمى هذا النبى فى جب السباع!! دفعتين فاقام فيه
prophesied and said: "The Messiah will come and will be killed; it will have no more savior after Him: Jerusalem will be ruined; after the death of the Messiah, the offerings and sacrifices are annihilated." All this was fulfilled. There was in Babylon an idol, whose daily maintenance was twelve measures of flour, forty sheep, and six amphorae of wine. King Cyrus (Kourach) the Persian (El-Fârisi) prostrated himself every day before it and believed that the idol ate and drank what was taken for it. Daniel maintained the opposite to him and informed him that the idol neither ate nor drank. The king became angry and questioned the priests of the idol. They said to him: "Bel (Bil) eats this." In the evening, they presented the food before the king and went out. Daniel had ashes brought and spread them in that place before the prince, then Cyrus sealed that place with his ring. The next day, he opened the door and did not find any food; he let out a great cry: "O Bel!" Daniel laughed and showed him the trace of the footsteps of men on the ashes. The king killed the servants and broke the idol. Daniel also killed the dragon that the people of Babylon worshipped. This prophet was thrown twice into the lions' den; the first


[p. 251] {#po.16.p251}
دفعة يوم وليلة! ودفعة ستة ايام والجب مطبوق عليه3 ولم يرمى لم4 شىة كلوه المدةة وجرسه الله8 سالما وحمل الله حبقوق النبى من يروشليم" ومعه غدا كان قد صنعه للحاصدين 9! فوضعه'! على الجب يابل وصعد دانيال واكل معه! وتعزى من كلام حبقوق وسكث دانال فى بابل الى السنة التى رجع فيا بنودًا اسراييل الى يروشليم! اليوم الرابع والعشرون منهة!
time he stayed there for a day and a night, another time for six days. It was closed with a lid, and during all that time, nothing was thrown to the animals to eat. God kept Daniel safe and sound and transported the prophet Habakkuk (Habaqouq) from Jerusalem with the food he had prepared for the reapers. He placed him over the cistern in Babylon. Daniel climbed up, ate with him, and was comforted by the words of Habakkuk. He remained in Babylon until the year when the Israelites (Banou Isräyil) returned to Jerusalem. TWENTY-FOURTH OF BARMAHAT (March 20).
انى 7اهذا اليوم تتيح الاب القديس انا مقارة الطريرك بمدينة8! الاسكندرية هذا القدين نشأ من ضيعة تعرف9! بشيرا فزهد فى هذا العالم من صغرا واشتاق الى سير الرهيئة فدخل الى جل شيبات وترهب بكنيسة القديس مقاربوس وانجح فى الرهنة وظهرت له دلائل صالحة فلما تنيح انا كسما اتفق رأى الاساقفة على تهدمة هذا الاب ققدم من غير ان بهوى
On this day died the holy father Anba Macarius (Magärah), patriarch of the city of Alexandria (El-Iskandaryah) (933-953). He was from a village called Chira; from his youth, he led an ascetic existence in the world and desired the monastic life. He entered the mountain of Scetis (Chihât), took the habit in the church of Saint Macarius (Magäryous), was happy in his convent life, and gave proofs of his virtue. When Anba Cosmas (Qosmä) died, the opinion of the bishops was in agreement to name this father patriarch.


[p. 252] {#po.16.p252}
ذلك ولما خرج من مدينة الاسكندرية قاصد الطلوع الى الديارة كالعادةة عبر على ضيعته التى ولد! فيها وكانت والدته فى الحياة وكانت بارة طاهرة خايفة من الله زاهدة فى مجد هذا العالم طالية4 مجد السماين5 محبة فيه" فطلع هذا7 الاب الضيعة يسلم؟ على والدته فلما سمعت به لم تخرج اليه ولما دخل الى الليت ووجدها" قاعدةٌ تغزل وبااأ قات اليه ولا سلمت عليه فظن انها ما عرفته فقال ليا انا ابنك لم لا تسلمى على فاجابته !1 اما انا فقد عرفتك واما انت فما اظن انك قد عرفت اسمك ولا13 نفسك فانا كنت اشتهى ابصرك ميتا ولا1 ابصرك1 بطريركاكً! لانك بالامس كنت مطلوب بخطلية نفك فقط وانت اليوم مطلوب بكل رعيتك ثم بكيا كلاهما فدخل كلامها فى قلبه كان فى كل يوم بتذكريةا ونهد فى سيرته واصلاح رعيته منها لهم بالقراءة والمواعظ محرا فى وضع يده على من يقدمه من الاساقفة والكبنة ولم يكن يتعرض لاحد شيئا من امزال
He was named without having desired it. When he left the city of Alexandria, with the intention of going up to the monasteries, according to custom, he passed through the village where he was born and where his mother was still alive: she was virtuous, pure, fearing God, disdaining the glory of this world and seeking that of the heavens, and loving Him. This father went up to the village to greet his mother. When she learned of his arrival, she did not go out to meet him, and when he entered the house, he found her sitting, spinning; she did not rise and did not greet him. He thought she did not recognize him and said to her: "I am your son; why do you not greet me?" - She replied to him: "As for me, I recognized you, but as for you, I do not believe that you know your name or your soul; I would prefer to see you dead and not patriarch, for yesterday, you were responsible only for your personal faults, and today you are responsible for your entire flock." Then they both wept, and this word penetrated his heart: every day, he repeated it to himself; he applied himself to his conduct and to perfecting his people by warning them through reading and preaching, taking every precaution when he laid on hands while consecrating bishops or priests; he never offered anyone a share of church goods nor of what


[p. 253] {#po.16.p253}
البيع ولا مما حرم عليه اخذه وكان لا! يقدم احدا الا بتزكية الكاهنة المباركين وكان مداوما توصية الاساقفة والقسوس برعاية الشعب3 وحراستهم4 بالمياس والمواعظ واقام" على الكرسى الرسولى عشرون سنة فى هدو وسلامة واللبعة؟ على .يده مستقيمة والصلوات7 والقداسات قائمة مستمرة ثم تنيح لام" صلاته معنا امين9 اليوم الخامس والعشرون من برمبات"!
it was forbidden to take; he consecrated no one except on the testimony of the blessed priests; continually, he exhorted the bishops and priests to watch over their flock and to guard it through homilies and sermons. He remained twenty years on the apostolic see, in peace and tranquility. The church was maintained thanks to him; prayers and offerings lasted without interruption. He died in peace. May his prayer be with us! Amen. TWENTY-FIFTH OF BARMAHAT (March 21).
فى “اهذ|ا البوم تيح "ا المعظم4! الرسولة1 اونيسوفورس6! الواحد من السبحيخ لمي الى انتخهم الرب هذا كان”! من بنى اسرايل من سبط مين ابن ابوين حافظين الناموس فتبع المخلص من حملة من تبعه لينظن اعماله فى عظم اياته وسمع تعليمه قلما اقام هكذا اباما8! وهو ينظر فى كل وقت ايات مخلفة اتفق انه كان معه حاضرا"! من
On this day died the venerable apostle Onesiphorus (A Ounisoufouros, B Ounisoufourous), one of the seventy disciples whom the Lord chose. This saint was of the Israelites (Banou Isräyil), of the tribe of Benjamin (Benyâmin), son of parents who knew the law by heart. He was one of those who followed the Savior to see his actions in the greatness of the miracles and to hear his teaching. When he had remained thus for some time, seeing at every moment


[p. 254] {#po.16.p254}
جملة المجمع عندما اقام الرب ابن الارملة بنابين قما تخ بعد هذد" الاية ولا طلب معها دليل اخر بل للحين3 ترك الاستضاء بسراج ناموس اليهودى وقصد شمس البر وتقدم الى الرب يسوع وأمن به من كل قلبه4 وقصد خلاص نفسه وتعمد من يده فلما قبل نمة المزى بعلية صبيون حيث كان صحة التلاميذ تبع التلاميذة وخدمم وش فى بلاد كثيرة فاقامه الرسل اسقفا على كوراتاس9 وش7 قيها وعمد اهليا واثار تقوم تعليمة ووعتله وكان؟ يشفى تفوسهم واجساميم ولما اتم سعيه وحصل كليل المجد تنيح؟ بعد ان وصل الى سعين سنة منها احدى وارعين سنة0! مسبحيا وسعة وعشرين سنة يهوديا ذكره الرسول بولس فى رسالته الى طيماناوى وفى غيرها وسلم عليه صلاته وشفاعته !! معنا امين
various miracles, it happened that he was present among all those who were there when the Lord raised the widow's son at Nain (Näyin). After this prodigy, he did not delay any longer and did not ask for any other proof, but, on the spot, he renounced being enlightened by the lamp of the Jewish law and turned toward the sun of truth; he advanced toward Our Lord Jesus (Yasou') and believed in him with all his heart; he sought the salvation of his soul and was baptized by his hand. When he had received the grace of the Comforter on the height of Zion (Sahyoun) while he was in the company of the disciples, he followed them, served them, and announced the Gospel in many countries. The apostles instituted him bishop at Kouratanäs; he preached there, baptized the inhabitants, enlightened their souls by his teaching and his preaching; he healed their souls and their bodies. When he had finished his struggle and received the crown of glory, he died after having reached seventy years, of which he spent forty-one in Christianity and twenty-nine in Judaism. The apostle Paul mentions him in his epistle to Timothy (Timâtäous) and elsewhere, and he greets him. May his prayer and his intercession be with us! Amen.


[p. 255] {#po.16.p255}
اليوم السادس والعشرون من برمهات!
TWENTY-SIXTH OF BARMAHAT (March 22).
فى هذا إليوم تنبحت القديسة الطوبانية ابراكسيه4 العذراء هذ" كانت ابنة اناس عظماء من .يت المملكة من مدينة رومية من جنس انوريوس؟ الملك فعند ناحة ايها املكها الملك على ابن بعض عثظماء الطارقة واتفق ان والدتها اتت الى ديار" مصر لتاخذ ما يتحصل"! ليها من احرةٌ رباع وغلات باتين!! خلفهم زوجا فاخذت هذا القديسة معها وكاات قد بلغت الى تسع سئين فلما وصلوا بلاد مص نزلا! يعض ديارات العذارى؟! الى ان ينزاة! اشغاليما وكانت رهانات ذلك الدير على غاية النسك ولا يأكلون شيئا من الزهومات ولا ذيتا ولا فاكبة ولا يذوقون خمرا ولا ينامون”! الا على الارض فاحبت الصبية الدير وانست8! الى الخديمة التى فيه فقالت لبا تلك الخديمة19
On this day died the holy, the blessed Euphrasia (Euphraxia) (B Abrâksyah, A Braksyah) the virgin. She was the daughter of considerable people of the imperial family of the city of Rome (Roumyah), and of the kinship of the emperor Honorius (B Anouryous, A Abouryous). Upon the death of her father, the emperor married her to the son of one of his principal patricians. It happened that her mother went to Egypt (Misr) to collect what was due to her from the price of fields and orchard harvests that her husband had left. She took with her this saint who had reached the age of nine. When they arrived in Egypt, they stayed in a convent of virgins, until they had brought their affairs to a successful conclusion. The nuns of this convent lived in extreme asceticism, eating neither fatty things, nor oil, nor fruits, not tasting wine, sleeping only on the ground. The young girl loved the convent and bonded with a servant who was there. The latter said to her: "Promise me not to leave this monastery anymore and not to


[p. 256] {#po.16.p256}
عاهدينى انك لا تعودى تخرجى من هذاةٌ الدين ولا تطلبى مليكك+ فعاهذدتها على ذلك فلما قضت؟ والدتها اشغالبا وقصدت” العودة الى بلادها طلبت ان تاخذ القديسة معها فلم تفعل وقالت انا قد انذرت واوققفت نفسى للمسيح ولا لى حاجة الى عرس هذا العالم؟ انا لى عرس حقيقى هو الرب9! يسوع المسيح فلما علمت !! والدتها؟! انها ما توافقها فى المضى معها فرقت هى! مالبا على المساكين واقامت معبا! فى الدير عدة سنلين ثم تنبحت فلما سيع الملك ان امها تتبحت سير! يطلب الصبية ليملكها”! فارسات تعرفه بانها قد انذرت نفسبا للمسيح8! ولا تقدر تخلف9! النذر فبكا الملك وتعجب من حكمتها مع صغر سنها ثم انها تعبدت تبّدا زائدا وكانت تصوم ومين يومين! ثم ثلالة ثم اربعة ثم اسبوعا وفى الاربعين يوم المقدسة لا تأكل طبيخا فصدها الشيطان وضريبا فى رحلبا ضرية اقامت متالمة منهاة زمانا كبيرا الى ان تحنن الرب عليها وشفاها وكان الرب قد انعم عليها باشفا المرضى وكان لها طاعة عظيمة للرئيسة وللخوات! فكانت محبوبة من الكل وفى بعض
seek your husband." She promised her. When her mother had finished her affairs and had resolved to return to her country, she wanted to take her daughter with her, but she refused and replied: "I have made a vow and I have consecrated myself to the Messiah; I have no need of a fiancé of this world; I have a mystical spouse, it is the Lord Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah." When her mother recognized that she would not accompany her on her journey, she distributed her fortune to the poor and remained with her in the convent for several years; then she died. When the emperor learned of her death, he sent to ask for the young girl to marry her. She made it known to him that she had vowed herself to the Messiah and that she could not contravene her vow. The emperor wept and admired her wisdom despite her young age. She practiced her devotions more and more, fasting every two days, then three, then four, then a week: during the forty consecrated days, she ate nothing cooked. Satan (Ech-Chaïtän) hated her and struck her on the foot with a blow from which she suffered for a long time until the Lord had compassion on her and healed her. God had granted her the favor of healing the sick. She had great


[p. 257] {#po.16.p257}
الليالى رأت الرئيسة كان قصورا مسوطة وأكاليل موضوعة فكانت! متعة؟ لمن هولاء؟ فقيل4 لبا لابراكسية ابنتك وهى تجى الان لبولاءة فقصت الام الرويا على الخوات واوصتهم ان لا يعلموا ركسية وان الرب اراد أن تنيح ابركسية؟ فمرضت بحمى7 قليل8 فاجتممت اليها9 الخوات والرئيسة والخديمة وسألوها ان تذكرهم ثم تنبحت بسلام فكوا عليها الخوات وحزنوا كثيرا لعدمهم تلك19 القديسة ثم تنبحت بعدها!! الخديمة صاحتها وعدها بيسير مرضت الام فجمعت الخوات وقالت لهم انظروا من تقيموها عليكم اما لانى انا ماضية الى الرب لان ابركسية شفعت فتى فاغلقوا عاتى الباب واذهيوا فلما اغلقوا الباب19 عليها واتوا باكرا ليفتقدوهاة! وجدوها قد تنبحت صلوات الجميع تحفظنا وتحرسنا من جميع ضربات العدو الشرير الى النفس الاخبر وارنا المجد دائما ابداة! امين
obedience to the superior and to the sisters and was loved by each one. One night, the superior saw immense castles and crowns prepared. As she wondered with astonishment for whom they were intended, a voice said to her: "For Euphrasia your daughter, and soon she will come there." She told the dream to the sisters and recommended that they not inform the saint, for the Lord wanted her to die. She fell ill with a slight fever; the sisters, the superior, and the servant gathered near her and asked her to remember them. Then she died in peace. The nuns wept for her and felt great sorrow at having lost her. Then, her companion, the servant, died after her, and shortly after, the superior fell ill. The sisters gathered around her and she said to them: "See whom you will put at your head; as for me, I am going to go to find the Lord, for Euphrasia has interceded for me: close the door on me and leave." When they had closed the door on her and they had returned early in the morning to visit her, they found that she was dead. May their blessings for all of us protect us and guarantee us against the blows of the wicked enemy until our last breath! Glory eternally to Our Lord! Amen.


[p. 258] {#po.16.p258}
اليوم السابع والعشرون من برمهبات!
TWENTY-SEVENTH OF BARMAHAT (March 23).
فى هذا اليوم تنيح الاب المغبوط سراج البرية اب جميع الرهبان القديس؟ ابو مقار العظيم4 هذا القديس كان من اهلة ششويرة من اعمال منوف كاناة ابويه صالحين بادين وكان ابو يسمى ابراهيم ولم يكن له ولدا فظهر له8 رويا من قبل الرب واعلمه ان الرب9 يرزقه ولدا يمون ذكر» شائعا0! فى!! الارض ويرذق بنون روحانين وعد قليل رزق هذا القديس وسماد مقارة الذى تفسيرهة! الطوبانى وكانت فيه3! نعمة الله4! من صغرا وكان طائما لابائه فلما كملت قامته اراد ابويه ان يزوحادة! وكان هو يكرا هذا فاغصبوا7! ان يفعل هواهم؟! وازوجود فلما دخل الى خجلته جعل9! نفسه مريضا فاقام 0" مدة! وهو كذلك وعد هذا طلب من ايه ان يطلق له المضى الى البرية ليتفسح قليل
On this day died the blessed father, the lamp of the desert, the father of all monks, Saint Abba Macarius (Magär). He was from the people of Chechouir of the districts of Manouf. His parents were virtuous and pious. His father was named Ibrahim. He had no child. A dream appeared to him from the Lord and informed him that God would reward him with a son whose fame would spread over the earth and that he would have spiritual sons. Shortly after, he was rewarded with this saint and named him Macarius (Magärah) which means blessed. The grace of God was in him from his youth; he obeyed his parents. When his growth was complete, they wanted to marry him. He was reluctant, but they forced him to do their will and they married him. When he entered the bridal chamber, he feigned illness and remained so for some time. Then, he asked his father to let him go into the desert to be relieved a little of his illness. Every day, he asked the Messiah in


[p. 259] {#po.16.p259}
من المرض! كان كل .يوم يسأل السيح فى صلاته ان يعمل له ما يرضيهة فلما صار فى البرية ابص رويا كان كارويم ذى5 ستة اجنحة قد مك9 يده واصعدة الى رأس الجبل7 واوراه كل البرية شرقا وغريا طولاة وعرضا وقال له إن الرب قد" اعطاك هذا الجل ميراثا لك ولبنوك! ولما عاد من البرية وجد الصبية قد مرضت وماات وهى عذداء!1 بحلها فشكر! المسيح كثيرا ثم مات ابويه بعد ذلك ففرق كلما خلفولا على الفقراء والمساكين وإما اهل ششويرةا لما رأوا طهر القديس وعفافهة! جعلونه نا عليهم فنواة! له موضح خارجة! البلد وكانوا ياتوا اليه ويتقربوا منه واقاموا له خديما”! برسم ما يحتاجه ديع عمل بديه8! فاتفق لصبية انها زنت9! مع شاب فكذبت30 على هذا القديس انه الفاعل ذلك با فاخذوا القديس وامتانولا والموا وضربو ” وهو صابر على ذلك! فلما حان وقت ميلاد الصبية فتعس عليها الولادة وقارت ان تموت فعلمت ان ما اصابها هذا" الا
his prayers to do with him what He would. When he was in the desert, he had a vision: it seemed to him that a cherub (Käroubim) with six wings took him by the hand, made him climb to the summit of the mountain, and showed him the whole desert, to the east and to the west, in length and in breadth, and said to him: "The Lord has given you this mountain as an inheritance for you and your sons." When he returned from the desert, he found his wife ill; she died a virgin in state; he thanked the Lord greatly. And to the unfortunate all that they had left. When the people of Chechouir saw the purity and chastity of this saint, they established him as priest among them and built him a dwelling outside the city. They went to find him and received communion from him. They provided him with a servant for his needs; he sold the work of his hands. It happened that a young girl fornicated with a young man and she falsely accused the saint of being the culprit. They seized him, they threw themselves upon him, they made him suffer, they struck him, he endured that. When the moment of childbirth had arrived for the young girl, it was excessively painful and she was near death. She recognized that this was happening to her only because of her lie against the saint. She Then his parents died; he distributed to the poor


[p. 260] {#po.16.p260}
بسب كذها على القديس فاقرت بزناها وكذيا على القديس فلما بلغ القدين ان اهل البلد يريدون! ان ياتوا ويستغفروا منه ذكر الرويا التى زاها فى البريةة ثم ظهرة له ذلك الشارويم ولم يزل يتقدمه الى ان اصعدد الى البرية بوادى هبيب ققال له للملأك7 يا سيدى حدد لى مكان8 اسكن فيه9 فاجابه لا ليلا تخرج من المكان الذى احددة لك فتكون مخالفا لقول الرب بل البرية كلها لك اى موضع اردث اسكن فيه سكن فى البرية الدخلانية موضع دير القديسين9! ولما اتوه القديسين ١! مكسيموس ودوياتيوسن سكنوا قريب منه فلما! تنيحوا امر الملك ان .ياتى الى المكان الذى هو اليوم دير وقال له ان ذلك المكان13 يدعى على اسم اولادك14 الروم وهو الذى يدعى الوم دير برموس م ان القديس عمل عبادات وحهادات كثيرة وكانوا الشياطين يحاريوه حربا عظيما فقام واتى الى الموضع الذى فيه انياا! انطونيوس ولما رالا من بعدآ! قال هذا اسراييلى حقا لا غش فيه ثم عرفه الفتال الذى للشياطين والبسه الاسكيم وطلب ان8! يقيم عند فلم بدعه"1 بل
confessed her fornication and her slander. When Macarius learned that the people of the city wanted to come to find him and ask his forgiveness, he remembered the vision he had had in the desert. Then this cherub appeared to him and did not cease to precede him until he had made him climb into the desert, to Wadi Habib. Then he said to the angel: "Determine for me a place where I will live." - He replied to him: "No, so that you do not leave the one that I will assign to you and that you do not contravene the word of the Lord; but the desert in its entirety is yours; inhabit it in such place as you will wish." He lived in the inner desert at the place of the convent of the saints. When the saints Maximus (Maksimous) and Domatius (Doumatyous) came to find him, they lived near him. When they died, the angel ordered him to go to the place where his convent is today and said to him: "This place will be called by the name of your sons, the Romans (Roum)"; it is the one that is called today Dair Barmous. Then he made many adorations, delivered many struggles: the demons waged a violent war against him. He rose and went to the place where Anba Anthony (Antounyous) was. When the latter saw him from afar, he said: "Here is a true Israelite (Israyili) without guile." Then Macarius made known to him his struggle against


[p. 261] {#po.16.p261}
قال! له يقيم كل اسان فى الموضع الذى اتخذ له الرب3 ثم انه مضى الى موضعة وسكن فيه وشاع ذكر هذا القديس" فى سائرة الارض واحرى الله على يديه ايات عظيمة منها ان صاحب8 انطاكية سر له ابنته وبا0! روح نجس فاتته!! بزى! صبى فعرنها انها بنت! ثم شفاهاً! وسيرها الى ابيباتًا وكانوا دفعوا له ذهاة! فلم ياخذد منهة! وكان فى8! اوسيم راهب19 قد اظل الناس يقول ان ليس قيامة !9 وكانوا يقهلواة قوله فاتى اسقف اوسيم وشكا الى القديس حال الشعب مع المتوحدة وسأله مسأة
the demons and, having received the cowl from him, he asked him to stay near him. Anthony did not permit him, but said to him: "Let each one remain in the place that the Lord has assigned to him." Then he left, returned to his dwelling, and remained there. He accomplished great miracles through his hands. Thus, the governor of Antioch (Antâkiyah) sent him his daughter in whom was an evil spirit. She came to him dressed as a young man; he recognized that she was a girl, healed her, and sent her back to her father. They offered him gold, but he did not accept it. There was in Bouchim (Aousim) a monk who was leading people astray by saying: "There is no resurrection." They accepted his words. The bishop of Bouchim came to find the saint, complained to him of the conduct of the people toward the monk, and begged him insistently to help him. When he had gone with him to this religious, he saw. The reputation of this saint spread throughout the whole earth and God


[p. 262] {#po.16.p262}
كثيرة! ان يفيثه فلما ان اتى معه الى المتوحد رأى ان فيه روح نج شيطان ولما خاطيه فى هذا قال اتى لا اومن ان الموتى يقومواة واشتبى4 ان تقيموا لى انسان من المقابر فصلى القديس وطلب من الرب فقام انسان من9 الكفار الاولين فامن المتوحد واما7 الذى اقامه فسأل القديس ان يعمد فعمده واقام عنده است8 سنين فتتيح ثم ان" القديس دخل الى البرية الجوانية ليعلم ان كان فها احدا قبله فرأى0! شخمين عرايا فخاف منهم لانه ظنهما!! شياطين فطمنا قبله وصليا قدامه الباتارهون دهيرة م ودعيلكا! باسمه فعرف انهماة! قديسين فتقصوا منه عن امور العالم فاجابهم ثم سألبما! ان كانا مردا من15 الشتاء ويحترًا"! فى الصيف فاحاباد”! ان الرب قد بردنا ولنا ارحين سنة لا تيرد ولا تحت فتارك منهما وعاد الى مكانه ولما كثروا الاخوة حفر؟! لهم بير وناها الى الوسط ولما نزل اليها القديس"" ليفتسل اردموها عليه الشياطين فجانًا الرهيان وشاله!
that the evil spirit of Satan was in him, and when he spoke to him about this, the other said to him: "I do not believe that the dead rise, and I desire that you raise one of them from the tombs." The saint prayed and implored the Lord: one of the ancient infidels returned to life, and the monk believed (in the resurrection). As for the one whom Macarius had raised, he asked the saint to baptize him; he was baptized and remained near him for six years, then he died. Afterward, Macarius entered the inner desert to see if there was anyone there before him. He saw two naked persons and was afraid of them, for he believed they were demons. They bowed before him, recited the Lord's Prayer, and asked him his name. He recognized that they were saints. They questioned him about the things of the world, and he answered them. Then he asked them if they suffered from the cold in winter and the heat in summer. They answered him: "The Lord has refreshed us, and it has been forty years since we have felt either cold or heat!" He received their blessing and returned to his dwelling. When the monks multiplied, he dug a well for them and walled it in the middle. When he went down to wash, the demons stoned him; the religious came to pull him out. When the Lord willed that he should die, he sent him the cherub


[p. 263] {#po.16.p263}
منها ولما اراد الرب نياحتة ارسل له1 الشارويم الذى كان يفتقده فقال له استعدّ فاننا ناتى اليك وناخذك ورأى الاب انطونيوس والاب بخوميوس وجماعة من القديسين واخذ القوات الى ان اسلم الروح وكان له سبعة وتسعين سنة وشهد القديس بنودة تلميذه انه رأى نفس الصديق عند صعودها الى السماء والشياطين يصيحون خافه قائلين غلبتنا يا مقاربون فقال3 الى الان فلما وصل4 باب السماء داخلا صاحوا باعلى اصواتهمة غلبتنا يا مقاريوس فقال تبارك الرب سوع المسيح الذى خلصنى من ايديكم وكان قد اضصى اولاده ان يخفوا جسده فاتوا اقوام من اهل شور بلده واعطوا” تلميذه يوحنا مال الذى كان القديس ينبيه9 ان لا يحب المال واراهم9! جسد القديس ابو مقار ثم اخذو!! الى بلدهم ونوا ه14 كنيسة وقام فيها نحو ا المأية وستين سنة الى ايام مملكة العرب بعد بناية القلالى4! طلعوا بجسد فاما يوحنا تلميذ لاجل محته للمال تجذم بعد نياحة الاب مقاريوس صلاته16 تكون معنا”! أمين
who visited him; he said to him: "Prepare yourself, for we are coming to take you." He saw Saint Anthony, Saint Pachomius (Bakhoumyous), and a crowd of saints, and received strength until he gave up the ghost. He was seventy-nine years old. His disciple Paphnutius (Babnoudah) saw the soul of this righteous man ascending to heaven, while the demons cried out behind him: "You have conquered us, Macarius." He said to them: "Until now." When he arrived inside the gate of heaven, they cried out with their loudest voice: "You have conquered us, Macarius." He said to them: "Blessed be Our Lord Jesus the Messiah who has delivered me from your hands." He had recommended to his spiritual sons to hide his body. The people of Chechouir, his country, came and gave his disciple John (Youhannâ) the gold that he had instructed him not to love, and he showed them the tomb of the saint. They carried him off to their city and built him a church where he remained for about one hundred and sixty years, until the time of the Arab domination, after the construction of the cells (?); his body was carried away. As for his disciple John, because of his love for riches, he was attacked by elephantiasis after the death of our father Macarius. May his prayer be with us! Amen.


[p. 264] {#po.16.p264}
وفه! ايضا صلب رنا يسوع المسيح بالجسد من احجل خلاصن العالم واظلمت الشمس. لمعاينتها خالقها معلقا بارادته محتجنا بالجسد المزكى وكانت مدةة ظلمتها ن الساعة النادسة الى الساعة التاسعة فلما كان فى هذد4 الساعةةً امال الدب رأسه واه واسلم7 النشضى ففارقت تلك9 النفى الجسد ومضت الى الجحيم متحدة باللاهوت وكات النفن فى الجحيم نفك المعتقلين9 وكان متعاليا فوق السموات حاضرا"! مع الاب والروح القدس فسحائه وحلت قدره النى فدانا!! بذاته وفتح لنا باب ملكوته فله البح والمجد والفوة و]العظمة الى ابد الابدين! امين
On this day also was crucified bodily Our Lord Jesus (Yasou') the Messiah for the salvation of the world. The sun darkened in sympathy for its creator, suspended voluntarily, hidden in a pure body. This darkness lasted from the sixth to the ninth hour. At that moment, the Lord bowed his head and gave up the ghost. The soul left the body and went into hell, united to the divinity: there it delivered those who were in chains. He ascended above the heavens, present with the Father and the Holy Spirit. Praise be to him; may his power which redeemed us by his person and opened for us the door of his kingdom be glorified: to him be glory, strength, and greatness for ever and ever! So be it.
اليوم الثامن والعشرون من برمهات
TWENTY-EIGHTH OF BARMAHAT (March 24).
فى هذا الوم تنيح ةا القديس قططين هذا كان ابوه يدعى قونسطا الملقب
On this day died Saint Constantine (Qostantin). His father was named


[p. 265] {#po.16.p265}
بالاخض وكانت امه تدعى هيلانة وكان قونسط! ملك على البرنطية وكان مكسيمانون ملكا على رومية وديقلاديانوس ملكا على انطاكية وارض مصر وكان هذا قوسطا على ملة البونانيين الا انه كان خيرا قليل الشر محا فى الخير وفيه حنة ورأفة فاتفق؟ انه مضى الى الرها؟ فرأى اهلانة فتزوج با وكات مسيحية فحات منه بهذا الملك قسطنطين ثم تركها فى الرها ومضى الى البرنطية فولدت قسطلطين ورته تربية حسنة وادبته بكل ادب8 وكانت تزرع فى قلبه الرحمة والحنة9 على المسيحيين وام تجس ان تعمده ولا اعلمته انها مسيحية فكبر وتفرس9! فقام من الرها وج الى ابيه ففرح به لما رأى من النعمة والحكمة !! والفروسية قملكه ونوجه ثم بعد سنين توفى ابيه فتسلم! المملكة جميعها ونشر العدل والانصاف وقطع عن الناس مظالم الملوك13 فطاعته! الناس واحبودة! فوصل خبر عدله الى ساثر البلاد فارسات اليه كابس رومية يسألوه ان ياتى ونجدهم من ظلم
Constantius (Qounstä) and was surnamed Chlorus; his mother was called Helena (Hilänah). Constantius reigned over Britain (El-Brintyah); Maximian over Rome (Roumyah); Diocletian (Diglâädyänous) over Antioch (Antakyah) and the land of Egypt (Misr). This Constantius was of the religion of the Greeks (El-Younänyin), but he was good and without malice, loving the good and having a penchant and inclination toward it. It happened that he went to Edessa (Er-Rohä); he saw Helena and married her. She was a Christian and became pregnant with this Emperor Constantine. Then he left her in Edessa and departed for Britain. She brought Constantine into the world, raised him well, and instructed him in all science. She sowed in his heart mercy and compassion for the Christians, but did not dare to baptize him and did not teach him that she was a Christian. He grew up, became a good horseman; then he left Edessa and went to find his father. The latter rejoiced to see the grace, wisdom, and skill in horsemanship that were in him: he made him emperor and crowned him. Then he died a few years later, leaving him the whole empire. He spread justice and equity, delivered the people from imperial injustices; the people obeyed him and loved him. His reputation for justice reached all countries. The great men of Rome wrote to him to ask him to come and save them from the tyranny of Maxentius


[p. 266] {#po.16.p266}
مكسيموس! فلما قرأ كتنهم" اغتم لبا ونا اصاهم4 وما هم فيه وقى متحرا متفحراة ماذا يصنع9 لينقذهم من حزنهم فبينما هو فى البلدانة فى نصف انيار رأى8 ظهر له صليب9 مركب من كواكب!! مضيّة وعليه مكتوب! بليوانية نكاس كاطاوطن طجنه جه وق0ر)! الذى تفسي هذا تغلب فتعحب بكونه4! غلب ضياء الفشس وتفكرة! فى المكتوب عليه ثم اوراه لوزدلا براه مملكته فتعجبوا ولم يعلموا ما السبب الموجب”! لظبوره وفى تلك الليلة ظهن له ملأك الرب! فى الرونا وقال له اعمل مثال العلامة التى رأيتها تغلب يا اعداك فلما انتبه تقوى قلبه ثم عمل9! صليبا وجعله فوق ندا وام ان يرسم به فى عود السلاح صلبانا وذلك فى سابع سنة من مملكته اللرنطية ثم جمع عسكرلة وخرج لنجدة اهل الرومية فوصل الخر الى مكسيموسن!؟ فل جيرا على البحر وعدا الى بن قسطنطين ثم التقيا وتحاربا كل لما تقدمت علامة انكس
(Maksimous). When he had read their letter, he became concerned about it, as well as about what was happening to them and the situation in which they were. He remained troubled, reflecting on what he would do to pull them out of their distress. In the middle of the day, he saw a cross appear composed of brilliant stars on which was written in Greek: Nikas kataoutin (en toutô nika), which means: "You triumph by it." He was astonished that this cross conquered the light of the sun and reflected on what was written upon it. Then he showed it to his ministers and the great men of his empire; they admired it without knowing the reason for its appearance. That night, an angel of the Lord appeared to him in a dream and said to him: "Make the image of the sign that you have seen; you will conquer your enemies." When he awoke, his heart was strengthened; then he made a cross which he placed above his flag and ordered the wood of the weapons to be marked with a cross: this happened in the seventh year of his reign in Britain. Then he gathered his troops and departed to the aid of the people of Rome. The news reached Maxentius; he made a bridge over the sea and crossed toward the country of Constantine. The two armies met and engaged in battle. Each time the sign advanced, the troops of Maxentius were defeated. While he was in the country,


[p. 267] {#po.16.p267}
عسكر مكسيموس! فقتل من عكر مقتلة عظيمة ثم هرب مكسيموس3 ببقية4 عسكرهن وركب الجر ليتحصن برئبية فانقطع5 به الجر وهلك كفرعون؟ ودخل قسطنطين الى رومية فاستقيله اهلها بالشموع والملإهى والتاجات المرصعة بالجواض وكانت7 شعراءهم يمدحون الصليب ونعتونه بخاص مدينتهم8 ثم عيدوا؟ الصليب سبعة ايام ولما ان استقر بالرومية تعمد0! واكثر عكر من يد القديس سلبسطرس البابا فى السنة الحادية عشر من مملكته وهى الرابعة من بعد ان ظهر الصليب المجيد فتملك حينئذ كانت !! مملكة مسيحية ثم ارسل الى سائر مملكته باطلاق المعتقلين من احجل؟! الايمان وان بكرموا المسيحيين ولا بيانوا وان يدفع للكبنة اموال البرابى ووقافها وان يرب احناد مسيحيين"! يروسوا على الذذين من الامم ورسم ان لا يعمل احد عملا! فى اسبوع الالام والذى بعدد كاوامر الرسل ثم ارسل هيلانة امه الى بيت المقدس فى ابتغى”! عود الصليب
there was a great slaughter of his soldiers; then he fled with the rest of his army and crossed over the bridge to fortify himself in Rome; but it broke and Maxentius perished like Pharaoh (Far'oun). Constantine entered Rome; the inhabitants came to meet him with candles, musical instruments, and diadems inlaid with precious stones. Their priests celebrated the cross and represented him as the savior of their city. The festivities lasted seven days. When he was established in Rome, he was baptized along with the greater part of his army by the Pope Saint Sylvester (Silbastros) in the eleventh year of his reign, the fourth since the appearance of the glorious cross. Then he reigned as a Christian. He sent orders throughout his empire to deliver the prisoners for the faith, to honor the Christians, not to despise them, to return to the priests the riches of the temples and foundations, to increase the number of Christian soldiers, and to give them positions above the pagans. He forbade working during Holy Week, and the week that follows, according to the prescriptions of the apostles. Then he sent his mother Helena to Jerusalem (Bait el-Magdis) in search of the wood of the Cross in which is prosperity. In the seventeenth year of his reign, he gathered the holy Council of the 318 fathers at Nicaea (Niqyah)


[p. 268] {#po.16.p268}
الذى كان به نجاحة وفى السنة السابعة عشر من مملكته جمع المجمع ” المقدس الائمأية وثمانية عش بنقية! بترتيب امور المسيحيين باحسن نظام واجودهة ثم اهتم بالبرنطية وناها مدينة عظيمة ودعاها القسطنطينية على اسمه ونسبها للسيدة وجملبا بكل الجمال الجسمانى والروحانى وذلك انه جمع لبا اجداد كثير من اجساد الرسل القدسين لما سار هذا" السير الصالح واتت نياحته تمرض بيقوميدية وتنيح با ثم حمل فى9 تابوت من ذهب الى مدينة القسطنطينية فخرج البطريرك والكبنة وسائر الشعب وتلقوا وجزوه بالصلوات والقرات والاغانى الروحانية ووضع فى هيكل الرسل القدسين7 وكانت مدة حياته خمسة وسعين سنة منها اثنين وثلاثين سنة ملك اللبا خمسة الف وثمنمأية وارسة وثاثين للعالم ولربنا المجد والقوة والعظمة وعلينا رحمته ونعمته الى الابد امين
to regulate Christian affairs in the most beautiful order. Then he occupied himself with Britain (sic) and built there a great city which, from his name, he called Constantinople (El-Qostantinyah). He consecrated it to Our Lady and adorned it with all sorts of material and spiritual embellishments; thus he gathered there many bodies of the holy apostles. When death approached, he fell ill at Nicomedia (Nigoumidyah) where he died. Then he was transported in a golden coffin to the city of Constantinople. The patriarch, the priests, and all the people went out to meet him, received him, and buried him with prayers, exhortations, and spiritual songs; he was placed in the Church of the Holy Apostles. The duration of his life was seventy-five years, of which he reigned thirty-two, the first of which is the year 5834 of the world. To God is glory, power, greatness; may his mercy and grace be upon us! Amen. After he had led this virtuous life,
اليوم التاسع والعشرون من برمهبات9
TWENTY-NINTH OF BARMAHAT (March 25).
انى !1 هذا اليوم كانت البشارة الممتلية! خلاص على يد الملاك الكرم ؟! جبراييل4!
On this day took place the annunciation full of salvation by the glorious angel


[p. 269] {#po.16.p269}
